Convolution functions:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```
For m=0? handle.
```
def fast_conv(a):
    n = len(a)
    M = 2*n-1
    Mf = next_pow2(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, Mf)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*A, Mf)[:M]
```
For cross:
```
def fast_cross(p, w):
    n=len(p); M=3*n-2; Mf=next_pow2(M)
    rp = p[::-1]
    R=np.fft.rfft(rp,Mf); W=np.fft.rfft(w,Mf)
    return np.fft.irfft(R*W,Mf)[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Check length: conv length (n-1)+(len(w)-1)+1 = n+(2n-2)+1=3n-2? Wait w length 2n-1. Enough slice [n-1:2n-1] length n. Good.

We can optimize convolution by using `np.fft.rfft` with length maybe next power of 2, but for n=16384, M=32767 -> 32768, cross M=49150 -> 65536. Good.

`compute_score_from_p(p)`: assume p length n, S=p.sum(); if S<1e-12 inf; c=fast_conv(p); return 2n max(c)/S^2. We can use for evaluation. To speed, convolution only; no w. Could compute c and S. For parent etc.

`lse_obj_x(x, beta)`:
- if len n==0 return inf.
- x finite? if not, return 1e100, zeros.
- p= soft_prob_from_x(x)
- c=fast_conv(p)
- q = 2.0*n*c
- qmax = q.max()
- scaled = beta*(q - qmax)
- # To avoid overflow in exp, scaled max 0, min maybe -beta*range. If beta*range > 700? scaled negative large okay underflow. np.exp(scaled) safe; max 0.
- es = np.exp(scaled)
- s = es.sum()
- if s==0: es = equal? (not possible because max exp=1)
- w = es/s
- score = qmax + np.log(s)/beta
- h = fast_cross(p, w)
- grad_p = 4.0*n*h
- gx = p*grad_p
- gx -= np.mean(gx)
- return score, gx
```

Projected exponential update:
```
def exp_update(p, grad_p, eta):
    # log p + ... natural gradient
    logp = np.log(p + 1e-300)
    logp -= np.max(logp)
    logp -= eta*grad_p
    # subtract mean? To preserve sum after normalize; not needed
    m=np.max(logp)
    q=np.exp(logp-m)
    q/=q.sum()
    return q
```

def resample_p(p, n_new):
    xp = np.linspace(0,1,len(p))
    xq = np.linspace(0,1,n_new)
    q = np.interp(xq, xp, p)
    q = np.clip(q,0,None); q/=q.sum()
```

import time, numpy as np

def _time_callback(xk):

        return True
    return False
```

FFT length cache:
```
_POW2_CACHE = {}
def get_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    key=m
    val = _POW2_CACHE.get(key)
    if val is None:
        val = 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
        _POW2_CACHE[key]=val
    return val
```

`softprob(x)`:
```
def softprob_from_x(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    xc = x - np.mean(x)
    m = np.max(xc)
    e = np.exp(xc - m)
    s = e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s == 0:
        return np.ones(len(x))/len(x)
    return e/s
```

`lse_objective(x, beta)`:
```
def lse_objective(x, beta):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x)
    n = len(x)
    p = softprob_from_x(x)
    c = fast_conv(p)
    q = 2.0 * n * c
    qmax = float(np.max(q))
    scaled = beta * (q - qmax)
    # scaled max 0. If beta large and q-max negative huge, exp underflows okay.
    e = np.exp(scaled)
    s = e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        # fallback uniform over all? e at max=1 so s>=1
        return qmax + 0.0, np.zeros_like(x)
    w = e / s
    lse = qmax + np.log(s) / beta
    h = fast_cross(p, w)
    grad_p = 4.0 * n * h
    gx = p * grad_p
    # project out constant direction
    gx -= np.mean(gx)
    # If any nonfinite, zero
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gx)):
        return lse, np.zeros_like(x)
    return float(lse), gx
```
Question: Since `lse_objective` takes x vector length n and uses len(x), but `minimize` calls with xk; okay. But if x is list? convert.

For beta high, `np.log(s)` where s large up to len(q)=2n; log fine.

`compute_score_p(p)`:
```
def compute_score_p(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n=len(p)
    S=float(p.sum())
    if S < 1e-300: return np.inf
    q = p / S
    c = fast_conv(q)
    sc = 2.0*n*float(np.max(c))/(S*S)
    return float(sc)
```
For p sum1 can skip normalization? Use generic.

`p_to_x(p, clip=40)`:
```
def p_to_x(p, clip=40.0):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    S=p.sum()
    if S<=0: p=np.ones_like(p)/len(p)
    q=p/S
    q = np.clip(q, 1e-300, None)
    x = np.log(q)
    x -= np.mean(x)
    x = np.clip(x, -clip, clip)
    # Maybe remove mean again after clip
    x -= np.mean(x)
    return x
```
If some q zero, log eps. Clip high to ±40. For n=16384, min p maybe 1e-12; log ~-27, okay. Use 60.

def central_binomial_p(n):
    c=np.ones(n)
    for i in range(n-1): c[i+1]=c[i]*(2*i+1)/(2*i+2)
    c/=c.sum()

```
def exact_polish_exponential(p, max_iters, deadline, tol=1e-8):
    p = p.copy()
    best = compute_score_p(p); best_p=p.copy()
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       n=len(p); c=fast_conv(p); q=2n c; qmax=q.max()
       # active smoothing: use softmax with beta maybe increasing
       beta_active = 50 + it*5? or fixed 100
       scaled=beta_active*(q-qmax); e=np.exp; w=e/e.sum()
       h=fast_cross(p,w); grad=4n h
       # try steps
       improved=False
       for eta in (0.5,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002):
           logp=np.log(p+1e-300); logp-=np.max(logp); logp-=eta*grad
           # center? softmax will center? For p sum, just normalize exp(logp)
           m=np.max(logp); q=exp(logp-m); q/=q.sum(); q=np.clip(q,0,None); q/=q.sum()
           sc=compute_score_p(q)
           if sc < best - 1e-12:
               p=q; best=sc; best_p=q.copy(); improved=True; break
       if not improved:
           # try smaller gradient scale? break
           if eta==0.002: break
    return best,best_p
```

def smooth_noise(n, rng, max_freq=20):
    noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(noise)
    k = np.arange(len(F))
    F[ max_freq+1:] *= 0.1
    F[0]=0

Let's think about gradient correctness with softmax recentering. We compute p=softmax(xc). Let xc = C x where C=I-J/n. p = softmax(xc). derivative p wrt xc = p ⊙ (I - 1 * p^T?) Detailed functions:

`normalize_p(a)`: returns nonnegative normalized sum1.
```
def normalize_p(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(len(a))/len(a)
    return a/s
```

`central_binomial_p(n)`:
```
c=np.empty(n); c[0]=1.0
for i in range(1,n):
   c[i] = c[i-1] * (2*(i-1)+1)/(2*(i-1)+2)
return normalize_p(c)
```

`power_p(n, gamma)`:
```
c=central_binomial raw? To avoid recompute, can create raw. But ok.
c=central_binomial_p(n) # normalized. Raising normalized to gamma changes sum; normalize.
p = c**gamma
return normalize_p(p)
```
If c has zeros? no.

`mix_p(p1,p2,t)`:
```
p=(1-t)*p1 + t*p2; return normalize_p(p)
```
If lengths differ, need resample.

if len(p)==n: return p.copy()

This interpolates last to first if extrapolate? Not. Could use periodic? Simpler use endpoint=True for both [0,1]. q=...; normalize.

```

`random_sinusoidal_p(n,rng, strength=0.3, max_freq=8)`:
```
x=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
base=np.ones(n)
for k in range(1,max_freq+1):
    amp = strength / (k + rng.random())
    phase = rng.uniform(0, 2*np.pi)
    base += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x/n + phase)
    base += amp*np.sin(...)? maybe one each.
base=np.maximum(base, 0.01)
return normalize_p(base)
```

p2 = normalize_p(c * (1.5-0.5*j)) # front boost?

return inits
```

 exact_polish_exponential implementation:
```
def exact_polish_exponential(p, max_iters=200, deadline=None, beta_start=100.0, beta_end=5000.0):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    p = normalize_p(p)
    best_sc = compute_score_p(p)
    best_p = p.copy()
    for it in range(max_iters):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        n=len(p)
        c=fast_conv(p)
        q=2.0*n*c
        qmax=float(q.max())
        sc = float(2.0*n*qmax) # since p sum1 maybe
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc=sc; best_p=p.copy()
        beta = beta_start + (beta_end-beta_start)*(it/max(1,max_iters-1))
        scaled=beta*(q-qmax)
        e=np.exp(scaled)
        s=e.sum()
        w=e/s
        h=fast_cross(p,w)
        grad=4.0*n*h
        focused improvement? If grad all finite.
        eta=0.1
        improved=False
        for _ in range(12):
            # update log p
            logp=np.log(p + 1e-300)
            logp -= np.max(logp)
            logp -= eta*grad
            logp_max=np.max(logp)
            qnew=np.exp(logp-logp_max)
            qnew_sum=qnew.sum()
            if qnew_sum <=0: break
            qnew=qnew/qnew_sum
            scnew=compute_score_p(qnew)
            if scnew < best_sc - 1e-12:
                p=qnew; best_sc=scnew; best_p=qnew.copy(); improved=True
                break
            eta *= 0.5
        if not improved:
            # If no improvement at smallest eta, maybe try opposite? No, break to avoid waste.
            # Could try a small random jitter? skip.
            if eta < 1e-4:
                break
    return best_sc,best_p
```
But `compute_score_p` normalizes again. p sum1. Good.

This may be slow if max_iters 200 and each no-improve tries 12_fft. We can reduce. Maybe use fixed eta schedule not repeating 12: `for eta in [0.1,0.05,...]` and break if improved, else continue next eta. That's max 8. Good.

Use beta active maybe high; smoothed active only near max. Beta end 1000? If too low, use many lags; step maybe effective. We can try beta schedule but one per iter.

Perturbations for local restarts:
```
def perturb_x(x, rng, smooth_scale, max_freq):
    noise = rng.standard_normal(len(x))
    F=np.fft.rfft(noise)
    k=np.arange(len(F))
    # low-pass: for k>max_freq, multiply 0.1
    F[k > max_freq] *= 0.1
    F[0]=0
    noise=np.fft.irfft(F, n)
    # center noise
    noise -= noise.mean()
    return x + smooth_scale*noise
```
Then p=softprob(x), score. Run L-BFGS short if promising. We can in phase3 do a few.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    hard_deadline = t_start + budget - 30.0
    # parent
    parent_p = None
    try:
       if 'height_sequence_1' in globals():
           hs = globals()['height_sequence_1']
           if hs is not None:
              parent_p = normalize_p(np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
    except Exception:
       parent_p = None
    lengths = []
    if parent_p is not None:
       plen = len(parent_p)
       if plen >= 1024 and plen <= 32768:
          lengths.append(plen)
    for n in [4096,8192,16384]:
       if not any(abs(n-l)<=32 for l in lengths):
          lengths.append(n)
    if len(lengths)>3:
       # remove smallest? keep largest? Maybe sort and keep 3 largest to maximize attack?
       lengths=sorted(set(lengths))
       lengths=lengths[-3:]  # keep largest, removes 4096 if 4
    lengths=sorted(lengths)
```

`smooth_noise(n, rng, max_freq=12)`:
```
noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
F = np.fft.rfft(noise)
k = np.arange(len(F))
if max_freq < len(F)-1:
    F[max_freq+1:] *= 0.05
F[0] = 0.0
noise = np.fft.irfft(F, n)
noise -= np.mean(noise)
# normalize scale to 1 std if nonzero
std = noise.std()
if std > 0: noise /= std
return noise
```
If n small, okay.

`random_blocked_p`:
```
def random_blocked_p(n, rng, num_blocks=16):
    if n<=0: return []
    num_blocks=max(2,min(num_blocks,n))
    per=(n+num_blocks-1)//num_blocks
    vals=0.2+0.8*rng.random(num_blocks)
    a=np.repeat(vals, per)[:n]
    # simple smoothing via box filter
    if len(a)>1:
       kernel=np.ones(3)/3.0
       a=np.convolve(a, kernel, mode='same')
    return normalize_p(a)
```

`build_initials` with no lambda:
```
def build_initials(n, rng, parent_p):
    inits=[]
    if parent_p is not None:
       ppar = parent_p if len(parent_p)==n else resample_p(parent_p,n)
       inits.append(ppar)
       inits.append(np.flip(ppar)) # normalize? flip same sum
    c=central_binomial_p(n)
    inits.append(c)
    inits.append(power_p(n,1.25))
    inits.append(power_p(n,0.75))
    inits.append(mix_p(c, c[::-1], 0.2))
    inits.append(mix_p(c, c[::-1], 0.5))
    x=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)/n
    inits.append(normalize_p(c*(0.3+0.7*x)))
    inits.append(normalize_p(c*(1.0-0.3*x)))
    for _ in range(3): inits.append(random_sinusoidal_p(n, rng))
    inits.append(random_blocked_p(n, rng, max(4, min(32, n//64))))
    if parent_p is not None:
       inits.append(normalize_p(ppar*(1.0+0.05*rng.standard_normal(n))))
    return inits
```
Could include >15; okay.

```
a = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
if a.size>0:

```
Good. `normalize_p` can be simpler but for parent use robust. We can also make `normalize_p` robust by scaling max.
```
def normalize_p(a):
   a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
   a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
   m = float(np.max(a)) if a.size else 0.0
   if m == 0.0: return np.ones(len(a))/max(1,len(a))
   a = a / m
   s = float(a.sum())
   if s <= 1e-300: return np.ones(len(a))/len(a)
   return a / s
```
No clip 1000 (not needed for internal). Final out clip to [0,1000] anyway. Good.

q=np.maximum(p,1e-300)
x=np.log(q); x-=np.mean(x); x=np.clip(x,-60,60); x-=np.mean(x); return x
```
Good.

```
def flat_l2_objective(x):
    if not finite...
    p=softprob_from_x(x)
    c=fast_conv(p)
    q=2.0*n*c
    r = q - 1.0
    # optionally small weight on negative r? no.
    val = float(np.dot(r,r))
    h=fast_cross(p, r)
    grad_p=8.0*n*h
    gx=p*grad_p; gx-=np.mean(gx)
    return val, gx
```

`random_sinusoidal_p`: Use `np.maximum(base, 1e-3)` to avoid zero.
```
def random_sinusoidal_p(n, rng):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    base = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(1,9):
       amp = 0.15 / (k + rng.random())
       ph1 = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*np.pi)
       ph2 = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*np.pi)
       base += amp * np.cos(2.0*np.pi*k*x/float(n) + ph1)
       base += amp * np.sin(2.0*np.pi*k*x/float(n) + ph2)
    base = np.maximum(base, 1e-3)
    return normalize_p(base)
```
Maybe amplitude too high causing zeros clipped; okay.

xq = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,endpoint=True)
q = np.interp(xq, xp, p)
return normalize_p(q)
```

Let's write `fast_cross` carefully:
```
def fast_cross(p, w):
    n = len(p)
    M = 3*n - 2
    Mf = get_pow2(M)
    rp = p[::-1]
    R = np.fft.rfft(rp, Mf)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, Mf)
    out = np.fft.irfft(R * W, Mf)
    return out[n-1:2*n-1]
```
If n=1, M=1, Mf=1, out length1, slice [0:1] okay.

def run_lbfgs_x(x0, beta, phase_deadline, maxiter, maxfun):
    global _GLOBAL_DEADLINE
    x0 = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if x0.size == 0: return x0
    if phase_deadline is not None and time.monotonic() >= float(phase_deadline):
         return x0
    x0 = x0 - np.mean(x0)
    x0 = np.clip(x0, -60.0, 60.0)
    n = x0.size
    bounds = make_bounds(n)
    opts = {'maxcor':50, 'maxls':50, 'ftol':1e-15, 'gtol':1e-12, 'maxiter':int(maxiter), 'maxfun':int(maxfun)}
    _GLOBAL_DEADLINE = float(phase_deadline)
    try:
       res = minimize(lse_objective, x0, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, callback=_time_callback, options=opts)
    except Exception:
       res = None
    _GLOBAL_DEADLINE = None
    if res is not None and hasattr(res, 'x'):
       return np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    return x0
```

`exact_polish_exponential` implementation with eta trials. Need not use `compute_score_p` if exact score can use c from qmax; but for trial need full score. Use `compute_score_p`. To speed, compute current sc from qmax before trials.

```
def exact_polish_exponential(p, max_iters=300, deadline=None):
    p = normalize_p(p)
    best_p = p.copy(); best_sc = compute_score_p(p)
    beta = 200.0
    for it in range(max_iters):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > float(deadline): break
        n=len(p)
        c=fast_conv(p)
        q=2.0*n*c
        qmax=float(np.max(q))
        sc=2.0*n*qmax
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc=sc; best_p=p.copy()
        beta = 50.0 + 50.0 * it
        if beta > 5000: beta=5000
        scaled = beta*(q-qmax)
        e=np.exp(scaled); s=e.sum(); w=e/s
        h=fast_cross(p,w)
        grad=4.0*n*h
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): break
        improved=False
        eta=0.2
        for _ in range(10):
            logp=np.log(p + 1e-300)
            logp -= np.max(logp)
            logp -= eta*grad
            lm=np.max(logp)
            qnew=np.exp(logp-lm)
            qs=qnew.sum()
            if qs <=0: eta*=0.5; continue
            qnew/=qs
            scnew=compute_score_p(qnew)
            if scnew < best_sc - 1e-12:
                 p=qnew; best_sc=scnew; best_p=qnew.copy(); improved=True; break
            eta *=0.5
            if eta < 5e-5: break
        if not improved:
            # Try a small random nudge? maybe break after several
            # To avoid immediate break, reduce beta? It already increasing.
            # If no improvement, break to save time.
            break
    return best_sc,best_p
```
If no improvement at eta=0.2, tries smaller. If still no, break. Maybe one no-improvement due local min; restarting perturbations handle. Good.

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative step function minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

Implementation ideas:
  - Work in log-softmax variables so probabilities stay positive and sum to 1.
  - Use a smooth LSE approximation of the max-convolution objective.
  - Continue from low to high LSE temperature to flatten the square convolution.
  - Maintain multiple lengths (including a parent if available) and several
    structured/random initializations.
  - After L-BFGS refinement, perform exact exponential-gradient polish and a
    few smooth-restart local searches.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_GLOBAL_DEADLINE = None
_POW2_CACHE = {}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Time callback for scipy.minimize
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _time_callback(xk):
    if _GLOBAL_DEADLINE is not None and time.monotonic() > float(_GLOBAL_DEADLINE):
        return True
    return False

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# FFT helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def get_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    val = _POW2_CACHE.get(m)
    if val is None:
        val = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
        _POW2_CACHE[m] = val
    return val


def fast_conv(a):
    n = len(a)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    Mf = get_pow2(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, Mf)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * A, Mf)[:M]


def fast_cross(p, w):
    """
    Returns h of length len(p), where
        h_i = sum_m w_m * p_{m-i}
    with p and w padded by zeros outside their valid ranges.
    """
    n = len(p)
    M = 3 * n - 2
    Mf = get_pow2(M)
    rp = p[::-1]
    R = np.fft.rfft(rp, Mf)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, Mf)
    out = np.fft.irfft(R * W, Mf)
    return out[n - 1:2 * n - 1]

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Normalization and scoring
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def normalize_p(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(a))
    if mx <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    a = a / mx
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    return a / s


def compute_score_p(p):
    """Score for a probability vector p (sum 1)."""
    p = normalize_p(p)
    n = len(p)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = fast_conv(p)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if cmax < 0.0:
        cmax = 0.0
    return 2.0 * n * cmax

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Softmax parameterization and smooth objective
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def softprob_from_x(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    xc = x - np.mean(x)
    m = np.max(xc)
    e = np.exp(xc - m)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(x.size, dtype=np.float64) / x.size
    return e / s


def lse_objective(x, beta):
    """
    Smoothed objective:
        LSE_beta(2*n*conv(p,p))
    and its gradient with respect to x, where p = softmax(centered x).
    """
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0:
        return 0.0, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)

    n = x.size
    p = softprob_from_x(x)
    c = fast_conv(p)
    q = 2.0 * n * c
    # remove small negative FFT noise
    q = np.where(q > 0.0, q, 0.0)
    qmax = float(np.max(q))

    scaled = float(beta) * (q - qmax)
    e = np.exp(scaled)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return float(qmax), np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
    w = e / s
    lse = qmax + np.log(s) / float(beta)

    h = fast_cross(p, w)
    grad_p = 4.0 * n * h
    gx = p * grad_p
    gx -= np.mean(gx)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gx)):
        return float(lse), np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(lse), gx


def p_to_x(p, clip=60.0):
    p = normalize_p(p)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    x = np.log(p)
    x -= np.mean(x)
    x = np.clip(x, -clip, clip)
    x -= np.mean(x)
    return x


def make_bounds(n, lo=-60.0, hi=60.0):
    return [(lo, hi)] * n


def run_lbfgs_x(x0, beta, phase_deadline, maxiter, maxfun):
    global _GLOBAL_DEADLINE
    x0 = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if x0.size == 0:
        return x0
    if phase_deadline is not None and time.monotonic() >= float(phase_deadline):
        return x0

    x0 = x0 - np.mean(x0)
    x0 = np.clip(x0, -60.0, 60.0)

    opts = {
        "maxcor": 50,
        "maxls": 30,
        "ftol": 1e-15,
        "gtol": 1e-12,
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
    }

    _GLOBAL_DEADLINE = float(phase_deadline)
    try:
        res = minimize(
            lse_objective,
            x0,
            args=(float(beta),),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=make_bounds(x0.size),
            callback=_time_callback,
            options=opts,
        )
    except Exception:
        res = None
    finally:
        _GLOBAL_DEADLINE = None

    if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x"):
        return np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    return x0

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Initializations
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def central_binomial_p(n):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    c[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        c[i] = c[i - 1] * (2.0 * (i - 1) + 1.0) / (2.0 * (i - 1) + 2.0)
    return normalize_p(c)


def power_p(n, gamma):
    p = central_binomial_p(n)
    p = np.power(p, gamma)
    return normalize_p(p)


def mix_p(p1, p2, t):
    p1 = normalize_p(p1)
    if len(p2) != len(p1):
        p2 = resample_p(p2, len(p1))
    p = (1.0 - t) * p1 + t * p2
    return normalize_p(p)


def resample_p(p, n):
    p = normalize_p(p)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if len(p) == n:
        return p.copy()
    xp = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, len(p), endpoint=True)
    xq = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=True)
    q = np.interp(xq, xp, p)
    return normalize_p(q)


def random_sinusoidal_p(n, rng):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    base = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(1, 9):
        amp = 0.18 / (float(k) + float(rng.random()))
        ph1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        ph2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        base += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + ph1)
        base += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + ph2)
    base = np.maximum(base, 1e-3)
    return normalize_p(base)


def random_blocked_p(n, rng, num_blocks=16):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    num_blocks = max(2, min(int(num_blocks), n))
    per = (n + num_blocks - 1) // num_blocks
    vals = 0.2 + 0.8 * rng.random(num_blocks)
    a = np.repeat(vals, per)[:n]
    if a.size > 1:
        kernel = np.ones(3, dtype=np.float64) / 3.0
        a = np.convolve(a, kernel, mode="same")
    return normalize_p(a)


def build_initials(n, rng, parent_p):
    inits = []

    ppar = None
    if parent_p is not None and parent_p.size > 0:
        ppar = parent_p if parent_p.size == n else resample_p(parent_p, n)
        inits.append(ppar)
        inits.append(ppar[::-1])

    c = central_binomial_p(n)
    inits.append(c)
    inits.append(power_p(n, 0.75))
    inits.append(power_p(n, 1.25))
    inits.append(mix_p(c, c[::-1], 0.15))
    inits.append(mix_p(c, c[::-1], 0.45))

    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    inits.append(normalize_p(c * (0.3 + 0.7 * x)))
    inits.append(normalize_p(c * (1.3 - 0.3 * x)))

    for _ in range(3):
        inits.append(random_sinusoidal_p(n, rng))

    inits.append(random_blocked_p(n, rng, max(4, min(32, max(2, n // 64)))))

    if ppar is not None:
        inits.append(normalize_p(ppar * (1.0 + 0.06 * rng.standard_normal(n))))

    return inits

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Exact exponential-gradient polish
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def exact_polish_exponential(p, max_iters=300, deadline=None):
    p = normalize_p(p)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = compute_score_p(p)

    for it in range(int(max_iters)):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
            break

        n = len(p)
        if n == 0:
            break

        c = fast_conv(p)
        q = 2.0 * n * c
        q = np.where(q > 0.0, q, 0.0)
        qmax = float(np.max(q))
        sc = 2.0 * n * qmax

        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_p = p.copy()

        beta = 50.0 + 50.0 * it
        if beta > 5000.0:
            beta = 5000.0

        scaled = beta * (q - qmax)
        e = np.exp(scaled)
        s = float(np.sum(e))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            break
        w = e / s

        h = fast_cross(p, w)
        grad = 4.0 * n * h
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            break

        gscale = float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
        if gscale > 1e-12:
            eta = 0.25 / gscale
        else:
            eta = 0.25
        if eta > 1.0:
            eta = 1.0
        if eta < 1e-5:
            eta = 1e-5

        improved = False
        for _ in range(10):
            logp = np.log(p + 1e-300)
            logp -= np.max(logp)
            logp -= eta * grad
            lm = np.max(logp)
            qnew = np.exp(logp - lm)
            qs = float(np.sum(qnew))
            if qs <= 0.0:
                eta *= 0.5
                continue
            qnew /= qs
            scnew = compute_score_p(qnew)
            if scnew < best_sc - 1e-12:
                p = qnew
                best_sc = scnew
                best_p = qnew.copy()
                improved = True
                break
            eta *= 0.5
            if eta < 5e-6:
                break

        if not improved:
            break

    return float(best_sc), best_p

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Smooth noise for restarts
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def smooth_noise(n, rng, max_freq=10):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(noise)
    k = np.arange(len(F))
    if len(F) > max_freq + 1:
        F[max_freq + 1:] *= 0.05
    F[0] = 0.0
    noise = np.fft.irfft(F, n)
    noise -= np.mean(noise)
    std = float(np.std(noise))
    if std > 1e-12:
        noise /= std
    return noise

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main search
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(120.0, float(budget_s))
    work = budget - 30.0

    phase1_end = t_start + 0.60 * work
    phase2_end = t_start + 0.85 * work
    phase3_end = t_start + 0.97 * work
    hard_end = t_start + budget - 6.0

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Load parent if available.
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    parent_p = None
    try:
        if "height_sequence_1" in globals():
            hs = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
            if hs is not None:
                parent_p = normalize_p(np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
                if parent_p.size == 0:
                    parent_p = None
    except Exception:
        parent_p = None

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Choose lengths.
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    lengths = []
    if parent_p is not None:
        plen = int(parent_p.size)
        if 2048 <= plen <= 24576:
            lengths.append(plen)

    for n in [4096, 8192, 16384]:
        same = False
        for l in lengths:
            if abs(int(l) - n) <= 32:
                same = True
                break
        if not same:
            lengths.append(n)

    lengths = sorted(set(int(x) for x in lengths))
    if len(lengths) > 3:
        lengths = lengths[-3:]

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # State.
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    best_sc = np.inf
    best_p = None

    # Quick fallback.
    if best_p is None:
        p = central_binomial_p(max(1024, lengths[0] if lengths else 4096))
        best_sc = compute_score_p(p)
        best_p = p.copy()

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 1: multi-length, multi-initialization LSE continuation.
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    phase1_total = max(0.0, phase1_end - t_start)
    if len(lengths) == 1:
        length_fracs = [1.0]
    elif len(lengths) == 2:
        length_fracs = [0.45, 0.55]
    else:
        length_fracs = [0.22, 0.34, 0.44]

    beta_schedule = [3.0, 8.0, 20.0, 50.0, 120.0, 300.0, 800.0, 2500.0]
    beta_weights = [1.0, 1.0, 1.2, 1.4, 1.6, 1.8, 2.0, 1.6]

    cum_len = 0.0
    for li, n in enumerate(lengths):
        length_start = t_start + phase1_total * cum_len
        cum_len += length_fracs[li]
        length_deadline = t_start + phase1_total * cum_len

        if time.monotonic() >= length_deadline:
            continue

        inits = build_initials(n, rng, parent_p)
        scored = []
        for idx, p in enumerate(inits):
            if p is None or len(p) == 0:
                continue
            p = normalize_p(p)
            sc = compute_score_p(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                scored.append((float(sc), idx, p))
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_p = p.copy()

        scored.sort()
        keep = 3 if n > 10000 else 4
        top_ps = [p for sc, idx, p in scored[:keep]]

        if not top_ps:
            continue

        length_window = max(0.0, length_deadline - length_start)
        if length_window <= 10.0:
            continue

        if len(top_ps) == 1:
            init_fracs = [1.0]
        elif len(top_ps) == 2:
            init_fracs = [0.60, 0.40]
        elif len(top_ps) == 3:
            init_fracs = [0.45, 0.30, 0.25]
        else:
            init_fracs = [0.40, 0.25, 0.20, 0.15]

        cum_init = 0.0
        for ii, p_init in enumerate(top_ps):
            init_start = length_start + length_window * cum_init
            cum_init += init_fracs[ii]
            init_deadline = length_start + length_window * cum_init

            if time.monotonic() >= init_deadline - 2.0:
                break

            x = p_to_x(p_init)
            x0 = x.copy()

            for bi, beta in enumerate(beta_schedule):
                now = time.monotonic()
                rem = init_deadline - now
                if rem <= 3.0:
                    break

                total_w = float(sum(beta_weights[bi:]))
                if total_w <= 0.0:
                    break
                share = rem * (float(beta_weights[bi]) / total_w) * 0.95
                share = max(2.0, min(share, rem - 1.0))

                beta_deadline = now + share
                fps = 700.0 * (4096.0 / float(n))
                maxfun = int(max(300, min(40000, share * fps * 1.4)))
                maxiter = int(max(50, maxfun // 25))

                x_new = run_lbfgs_x(x, float(beta), beta_deadline, maxiter, maxfun)
                p_new = softprob_from_x(x_new)
                p_new = normalize_p(p_new)
                sc_new = compute_score_p(p_new)
                if sc_new < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc_new
                    best_p = p_new.copy()

                x = x_new
                # If no movement, stop this continuation.
                if np.allclose(x, x0, atol=1e-12, rtol=0.0):
                    x0 = x.copy()
                    if time.monotonic() >= beta_deadline - 0.5:
                        break

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 2: high-temperature refinement of the current best.
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    if best_p is not None and time.monotonic() < phase2_end:
        n = int(best_p.size)
        x = p_to_x(best_p)
        betas2 = [2500.0, 6000.0, 15000.0]
        weights2 = [1.0, 1.0, 1.0]

        for bi, beta in enumerate(betas2):
            now = time.monotonic()
            rem = phase2_end - now
            if rem <= 5.0:
                break
            total_w = float(sum(weights2[bi:]))
            if total_w <= 0.0:
                break
            share = rem * (float(weights2[bi]) / total_w) * 0.95
            share = max(5.0, min(share, rem - 1.0))
            deadline = now + share

            fps = 650.0 * (4096.0 / float(n))
            maxfun = int(max(500, min(60000, share * fps * 1.4)))
            maxiter = int(max(80, maxfun // 25))

            x_new = run_lbfgs_x(x, float(beta), deadline, maxiter, maxfun)
            p_new = normalize_p(softprob_from_x(x_new))
            sc_new = compute_score_p(p_new)
            if sc_new < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_new
                best_p = p_new.copy()
            x = x_new

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 3: exact polish, symmetries, and short smooth restarts.
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    if best_p is not None:
        ph3_start = time.monotonic()
        ph3_window = max(0.0, phase3_end - ph3_start)

        if ph3_window > 20.0:
            pol_deadline = ph3_start + 0.60 * ph3_window
            sc_pol, p_pol = exact_polish_exponential(
                best_p,
                max_iters=500,
                deadline=pol_deadline,
            )
            if sc_pol < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_pol
                best_p = p_pol.copy()

        # Cheap symmetrized/geometric combinations.
        p = best_p
        cand = normalize_p(0.5 * (p + p[::-1]))
        sc = compute_score_p(cand)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_p = cand.copy()

        p = best_p
        prod = np.maximum(p, 0.0) * np.maximum(p[::-1], 0.0)
        cand = normalize_p(np.sqrt(prod))
        sc = compute_score_p(cand)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_p = cand.copy()

        # Smooth restarts from current best.
        if time.monotonic() < phase3_end - 20.0:
            xbest = p_to_x(best_p)
            n = int(best_p.size)
            nrestarts = 8
            for r in range(nrestarts):
                now = time.monotonic()
                rem = phase3_end - 20.0 - now
                if rem <= 15.0:
                    break
                allowed = max(8.0, rem / float(nrestarts - r))

                scale = 0.07 + 0.12 / float(r + 1)
                noise = smooth_noise(n, rng, max_freq=10)
                xpert = xbest - np.mean(xbest) + scale * noise
                xpert = np.clip(xpert, -50.0, 50.0)

                # Two short LSE refinements.
                for beta, frac in [(800.0, 0.45), (3000.0, 0.55)]:
                    now = time.monotonic()
                    rem = phase3_end - 20.0 - now
                    if rem <= 2.0:
                        break
                    share = max(2.0, min(allowed * frac, rem - 1.0))
                    deadline = now + share

                    fps = 650.0 * (4096.0 / float(n))
                    maxfun = int(max(300, min(25000, share * fps * 1.4)))
                    maxiter = int(max(40, maxfun // 25))

                    xpert = run_lbfgs_x(xpert, float(beta), deadline, maxiter, maxfun)
                    p_new = normalize_p(softprob_from_x(xpert))
                    sc_new = compute_score_p(p_new)
                    if sc_new < best_sc:
                        best_sc = sc_new
                        best_p = p_new.copy()

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final short polish if time remains.
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    if best_p is not None and time.monotonic() < hard_end - 25.0:
        x = p_to_x(best_p)
        deadline = hard_end - 8.0
        n = int(best_p.size)
        fps = 650.0 * (4096.0 / float(n))
        rem = max(5.0, hard_end - 8.0 - time.monotonic())
        maxfun = int(max(500, min(30000, rem * fps * 1.2)))
        maxiter = int(max(80, maxfun // 25))
        x = run_lbfgs_x(x, 8000.0, deadline, maxiter, maxfun)
        p_final = normalize_p(softprob_from_x(x))
        sc_final = compute_score_p(p_final)
        if sc_final < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_final
            best_p = p_final.copy()

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Sanitize and verify.
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    out = np.asarray(best_p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if out.size == 0:
        out = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0

    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(out))
    if s < 0.01:
        out = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        s = 1.0
    out = out / s

    verified = np.inf
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in out]))
    except Exception:
        verified = best_sc

    print("=" * 60)
    print(f"internal best score : {best_sc:.10f}")
    print(f"evaluated score     : {verified:.10f}")
    print(f"final length        : {out.size}")
    print(f"elapsed             : {time.monotonic() - t_start:.1f}s / {budget_s:.1f}s")
    print("=" * 60)

    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
